   <section class="bench" id="accelerated-md-aladatom" data-problem="accelerated-md/aladatom">
   <h2>aladatom</h2>
   <p>Ranked by the v2 PAR-2 mean of the harness total. Only the newest full version of a template is in this table. A partial row, including a one-start asv smoke run, is not.</p>
   <table data-board-table="ranked" data-problem="accelerated-md/aladatom">
   <caption>Lower PAR-2 is better. PAR-2 and the call columns are harness force-call totals. Wilson bounds are the 95% interval on the success rate.</caption>
   <thead><tr><th>Template</th><th>Version</th><th>Commit</th><th>Source</th><th>Current</th><th>PAR-2</th><th>Success</th><th>Wilson low</th><th>Wilson high</th><th>Median calls</th><th>Mean calls</th><th>Failures</th><th>n</th></tr></thead><tbody>
   <tr><td colspan="13">No full harness row.</td></tr>
   </tbody></table>
   <p class="bench-gaps">Partial rows stay off the ranked table.</p>
   <h3>Partial</h3><ul>
   <li>eon-client/amd-hyper eOn campaign n=1, result file sets partial true.</li>
   <li>eon-client/amd-prd eOn campaign n=1, result file sets partial true.</li>
   <li>eon-svn/amd-hyper Eon svn campaign n=1, result file sets partial true.</li>
   <li>eon-svn/amd-prd Eon svn campaign n=1, result file sets partial true.</li>
   </ul>
   </section>
